The dashboard calendar computes the drop target from the droppable's ID ("YYYY-MM-DD:HH:staffId") and forces the slot's new start time to that exact grid cell, not wherever the pointer happened to be. The two reorderable lists (service catalog order, discovery-admin image order) snap to a sortable index via react-beautiful-dnd / dnd-kit. I grepped the whole web app for any free/continuous 2D drag (photo repositioning, a moodboard, a floor-plan canvas) and found none: the only other drag primitives are the sheet drag-dismiss and SearchMorph, both LOCKFILE-governed 1-axis GESTURE springs (position + velocity), not open-canvas placement. Solen is a booking marketplace, not an authoring tool, so it has no genuine 'CANVAS/FREE' surface to contrast against the snap behavior, it correctly has zero of them.
- Law evidence: _design-system/LOCKFILE.md:1427-1461 (SCOPE + §16.5) reserves free/velocity-driven release exclusively for pointer-gesture surfaces (sheet, SearchMorph), not for data-editing drags; no LOCKFILE or TASTE_LOG row proposes a freeform canvas anywhere in the product.
- Code evidence: app/[locale]/dashboard/calendar/page.tsx:548-565 (onDragEnd parses destId into dateStr/hourStr/staffId and snaps starts_at to that exact cell) + app/[locale]/dashboard/services/page.tsx:519-527 and app/[locale]/dashboard/discovery-admin/page.tsx:16 (SortableContext/rectSortingStrategy, index-snap reorder); grep for free-drag (`onPointerDown.*drag`, `useDrag`, unconstrained `drag=` on framer-motion) across app/components/lib returned nothing beyond the axis-constrained testimonial carousel.

### dm-drop-zone-line-vs-fill , Drop zones preview themselves: insertion line vs. column fill
- Reasoning: The two drop-target shapes Solen has both already preview themselves before release, using the mechanic appropriate to their shape. The calendar's cells are literally 'whole column' targets (an hour x staff slot), and they fill-highlight live (`snapshot.isDraggingOver`) the instant a drag enters, before mouseup. The two ordered lists (services, discovery-admin) are 'between items' targets; instead of drawing a static insertion line they use the standard library reflow (react-beautiful-dnd / dnd-kit physically shift sibling rows to open the gap in real time), which communicates the same pre-release consequence more concretely than a line would, you see the actual resting position, not an abstract marker. Given LOCKFILE's standing no-decorative-artifact stance, adding a literal drawn line on top of an already-working reflow preview would be redundant chrome, not new information.
- Law evidence: CLAUDE.md taste rule 2 (no decorative artifacts, every element must carry information or get deleted) argues against bolting on a static line where reflow already discloses the same outcome.
- Code evidence: app/[locale]/dashboard/calendar/page.tsx:934,1016,1049 (`snapshot.isDraggingOver ? "bg-s-coral/10" : ...` fill preview on the droppable cell, live during drag) and app/[locale]/dashboard/services/page.tsx:519-527 (react-beautiful-dnd Draggable/Droppable list with default reflow + `snapshot.isDragging` highlight, no static line needed).

### dm-shimmer-follows-reading-direction , Shimmer sweep follows reading direction
- Reasoning: The canonical, currently-locked shimmer primitive already sweeps left-to-right, matching reading direction for all four Solen locales (de/en/fr/it are all LTR, so there's no RTL case to branch on). Working the CSS background-position math: with `bg-[length:200%_100%]` and `bg-gradient-to-r`, the Tailwind `animate-shimmer` keyframe (backgroundPosition -200%0 to 200%0) sweeps the highlight band left-to-right, exactly the principle's claim, and Skeleton.tsx explicitly adopted it over the older CSS class specifically to be the one true token-aligned version. Worth flagging in passing (not a verdict-changer): the legacy `.skeleton-shimmer` utility class in globals.css animates the opposite direction (200%0 to -200%0, right-to-left) and is still referenced live in SearchTemplate.tsx, so there IS a real, currently-shipping instance that violates this exact principle, it's just the deprecated path, not the canonical one the design system already committed to.
- Law evidence: components/primitives comment (Skeleton.tsx) documents `animate-shimmer` as the intentional replacement for the legacy `.skeleton-shimmer` class 'because that class hardcodes its own gradient...which would override our tokens'; no LOCKFILE/MOTION.md row calls out sweep direction explicitly, but the shimmer pattern itself is locked (LOCKFILE:785, MOTION.md:96).
- Code evidence: tailwind.config.js:340,346-348 (`animate-shimmer` keyframe: -200%0 -> 200%0, left-to-right) used by app/[locale]/_components/primitives/Skeleton.tsx:83; CONTRAST: app/globals.css:557-560 (`@keyframes skeletonShimmer`: 200%0 -> -200%0, right-to-left, the reversed direction) still referenced live at app/[locale]/_components/search/SearchTemplate.tsx:257,262-264,1438 via the legacy `.skeleton-shimmer` class.

### dm-dropzone-answers-back-in-real-time , A dropzone must answer back in real time
- Reasoning: The shared, canonical upload component already does almost exactly what the principle describes: the instant a file enters the zone, `onDragOver` flips `isDragOver` and the zone visibly changes, border goes from neutral hairline to accent, background tints, and the icon disc darkens, all before the drop. That is 2 of the 3 signals (border + glow) fully wired; the 3rd (copy swapping to something like 'Release to upload') is not implemented, static title/hint text stays the same regardless of drag state. That is a real, minor incremental gap on an otherwise-shipped pattern, not a case for inventing something new. What IS a genuine problem, and a reuse violation rather than a design gap, is that two admin screens rolled their own dashed-border dropzone instead of reusing this component: they even promise drag-and-drop in copy ('Klicken oder Datei hier ablegen' / 'Click or drop a file here') but have zero onDragOver/onDragLeave/onDrop wiring, so dragging a file over them does nothing until release. Per CLAUDE.md's exists-check/reuse mandate, the fix there is 'adopt ImageUpload', not 'design a new dropzone-feedback pattern'.
- Law evidence: CLAUDE.md exists-check protocol (research before building a new system, reuse/extend what exists) directly governs the two outlier screens; LOCKFILE rule 2/CLAUDE.md rule 1 (no fabricated affordance) is relevant to the copy promising drop-support the two admin zones don't actually deliver in real time.
- Code evidence: components-legacy/ui/ImageUpload.tsx:321-342 (onDragOver -> setIsDragOver(true) drives border-s-accent + bg-s-ink/5 + darker icon disc, live before drop), used at app/[locale]/onboarding/salon/page.tsx:322 and app/[locale]/dashboard/ai-limits-admin/page.tsx; MISSING at app/[locale]/dashboard/verification/page.tsx:102-116 and app/[locale]/dashboard/discovery-admin/page.tsx:376-386 (both plain dashed `<div>`/`<input type=file>` with no onDragOver/onDragLeave handlers, despite messages/de.json:2221 'Klicken oder Datei hier ablegen' promising the gesture).

### dm-honest-determinate-progress-eta-speed , Honest determinate progress: percentage + time left + speed
- Reasoning: The 'determinate percentage over blind spinner' half of this claim is already shipped on the product's only real file-transfer surface: every image upload renders a real percentage-driven bar (10 -> 30 -> 50/80 -> 90 -> 95 -> 100, tied to actual resize/upload milestones, not a fake timer) instead of an indeterminate spinner. The ETA + transfer-speed elaboration genuinely does not exist anywhere, but that's correctly absent rather than a gap: photo uploads (client-resized to max 2000px, 10MB cap) finish in low single-digit seconds, never accumulating 'enough samples' for a rate/ETA readout to mean anything, and the only multi-item operation with real duration (the admin-only TikTok bulk importer) already shows an honest done/total counter rather than fabricating a percentage or a spinner. Booking, checkout, and search are all sub-second network calls. Given LOCKFILE's no-fabricated-data rule and the calm-not-loud restraint this product runs on, building a speed/ETA sampling layer for operations that finish before a human could read the number would be manufacturing precision nobody needs, this is a DONT_ADD on the elaboration layered onto an ALREADY_EXISTS core.
- Law evidence: CLAUDE.md taste rule 1 (no fabricated data, never render a number not wired to a live source) directly argues against inventing an ETA/speed readout for operations too short to sample meaningfully; RATIONALE.md:738 (Harrison 2010, progress-bar animation affects perceived duration) is the only law-side support and it's already satisfied by the existing real bar.
- Code evidence: components-legacy/ui/ImageUpload.tsx:88-129 (setProgress ticks 10/30/50-80/90/95/100, rendered as a real width-driven bar at lines 265-273) is the only file-transfer UI in the app; app/[locale]/dashboard/discovery-admin/page.tsx:248,262,282 (TikTok bulk import shows an honest done/total counter, not a fake spinner); grep across app/components/lib for onUploadProgress, ProgressEvent, XMLHttpRequest, or any speed/ETA calculation returned zero hits.

### dm-fitts-law-size-and-distance , Fitts's Law: size and place targets in the thumb zone
- Reasoning: Both halves of Fitts's Law (W = size, D = distance) are already locked and built. The 44px touch floor (W) is a design-contract row and cited as a deliberate Fitts/WCAG-AAA/HIG choice above the legal 24px minimum. The distance half (bottom-anchored primary CTAs on mobile) is live across the entire booking funnel, not just noted as a goal: every step of the wizard puts its primary commit action in a fixed bottom bar reachable by thumb. For a Swiss booking marketplace where the whole conversion funnel is mobile checkout, this is exactly right: the commit action (Weiter/Buchen/Bezahlen) needs to survive scroll and stay in the same physical spot step to step, which is what a bottom-fixed bar does. Nothing to add; if anything RATIONALE.md's own line calling this a 'retrofit target' is stale and should be updated to point at the shipped code.
- Law evidence: _design-system/RATIONALE.md:63-66 (Fitts's Law domain, 44px floor + 'Retrofit target: bottom-anchored primary CTAs on mobile'); _design-system/LOCKFILE.md design-contract row 'icon-button h-11 w-11'
- Code evidence: components-legacy/booking/PayConfirmStep.tsx:598 (fixed bottom-0 CTA bar, 'Buchen · CHF <total>'), StaffStep.tsx:180, DateTimeStep.tsx:266, ServicesStaffStep.tsx:543 (all fixed bottom-0 primary bars across the wizard); app/[locale]/_components/salon/SalonMobileBookBar.tsx:31 (fixed bottom-0 PDP book bar)

### dm-slider-hit-area-full-row , Slider drag hit area should be the whole row
- Reasoning: The two custom sliders in the product (price max-slider and rating bar in the search filter sheet) already bind pointer capture to the full track div, not the thin painted line. The code comment even states the intent in the exact words of the principle: 'Drag the whole track; commits on release.' This is correct for a filter sheet used one-handed while scrolling results: a 4px hit target would make the price cap and rating filters miss-prone on exactly the surface (mobile search refinement) where users drag fastest and least carefully. No gap to fill.
- Law evidence: none (no LOCKFILE row for slider hit-area; governed by the general Fitts/44px floor domain in RATIONALE.md, which this pattern already satisfies on the width axis)
- Code evidence: app/[locale]/_components/search/FilterSheet.tsx:113-131 (PriceSlider: trackRef div carries onPointerDown/Move/Up, role=slider, full w-full width, comment 'Drag the whole track; commits on release'); FilterSheet.tsx:204-237 (RatingBar, same pattern)

### dm-tone-brand-voice-empty-error , Tone = brand voice: empty and error copy must sound human
- Reasoning: This is fully locked and built, not a gap. SOURCE.md pins the exact empty/error copy patterns in the product's own du-form voice ('Etwas ist schiefgelaufen', 'Andere Stadt wählen') and explicitly bans the apologetic/technical failure mode the principle warns about ('Apologetic empty states ("Sorry, no results found")' is on the banned list). RATIONALE.md has a dedicated voice/tone domain grounding the register in NN/g's tone dimensions. The componentized ErrorState/EmptyState primitives enforce the human title+message+action shape at the type level (title/message/onRetry/retryLabel props, no room for a bare error code to leak through), and they're wired across dozens of real dashboard and customer surfaces via i18n keys, not hardcoded per-page copy. For a calm-not-loud brand, this consistency matters more than for most products: a stray 'ERROR 404' would break the register the whole rest of the app works to hold.
- Law evidence: _design-system/SOURCE.md:708-721 (§10.2 empty state / §10.3 error state patterns, explicit ban on apologetic copy); _design-system/SOURCE.md:1131-1132 (§18 brand voice table: Empty state / Error state rows with exact copy); _design-system/RATIONALE.md:792-796 (domain 22, NN/g tone dimensions + voice-constant/tone-varies-by-state)
- Code evidence: components-legacy/ui/ErrorState.tsx:20-32 (title/message/onRetry/retryLabel props, structurally prevents raw error-code leakage); components-legacy/ui/EmptyState.tsx and DiscoveryEmptyState/DiscoveryErrorState components; live call sites e.g. app/[locale]/dashboard/all-salons/page.tsx:208, dashboard/calendar/page.tsx:641, all wired through next-intl t() keys, not hardcoded strings

### dm-roach-motel-exit-parity , Roach Motel: exit must be as easy as entry
- Reasoning: For a booking marketplace, this is table stakes trust hygiene, not a growth feature: a customer who has to fight to cancel will simply never book with that salon (or Solen) again, which costs far more than the one retained booking. Measured against the actual flows, exit is already EQUAL OR EASIER than entry, not harder. Booking is a locked 4-step wizard (services -> Staff -> Zeit -> Bezahlen, project_booking_flow_canonical memory + BookingWizard.tsx). Self-cancel is 2 taps: tap 'Stornieren' on the card (handleCancel in BookingsList.tsx:58) opens CancelBookingSheet.tsx, tap 'Ja, stornieren' (confirmCancelBtn) to POST /api/bookings/[id]/cancel. Notification opt-in and opt-out use the identical single Switch tap in SettingsForm.tsx:215-220, no separate unsubscribe flow. The walk-in queue mirrors this: pay-in -> get a number is 2-3 steps, cancel is 'cancelConfirm' in one tap (queue/[token]/page.tsx). No evidence of buried settings, hidden menus, or extra steps guarding any exit path.
- Law evidence: No dedicated numbered law names step-parity explicitly, but PSYCHOLOGY.md hard line 5 ('No lock/blur walls before delivered value; no forced account before pay') and the guest-first law 4 embody the same anti-friction ethic applied to entry; the codebase already generalizes it to exit.
- Code evidence: components-legacy/booking/BookingsList.tsx:58-80 (2-tap cancel: card tap -> sheet -> confirm); components-legacy/booking/CancelBookingSheet.tsx:154-171 (single sheet, two stacked buttons, no nested menu); app/[locale]/profile/settings/SettingsForm.tsx:215-220 (one Switch for both opt-in/opt-out); app/[locale]/queue/[token]/page.tsx:46-47 (cancelConfirm one-tap)

### dm-confirm-shaming , Confirm shaming: never word the decline option to shame the user
- Reasoning: Confirm-shaming is explicitly named and banned in the product's own psychology law, not just accidentally absent. For Solen's 'calm not loud' DNA, guilt-tripping copy would be the opposite of restraint: it manufactures a manipulative emotional cost on a decision (keep a booking, decline a notification, skip a tip) that should be read as neutral information, not a morality test. Every decline/dismiss string checked across the codebase is plain and symmetric with its accept counterpart: 'Keep' / 'Behalten' next to 'Yes, cancel' / 'Ja, stornieren' (no plain-word shame), 'No tip, thanks' / 'Kein Trinkgeld, danke' for the tip prompt, and every 'No'/'Nein' in messages/en.json and messages/de.json (checked 3 occurrences) stays a single neutral word, never a self-deprecating sentence.
- Law evidence: PSYCHOLOGY.md law 10 (line 43): 'Banned: loss-framing invented stakes, countdowns on trivia, dishonest dismiss copy.' Dishonest/guilt-worded dismiss copy is a named hard-line violation.
- Code evidence: components-legacy/booking/CancelBookingSheet.tsx:164-171 (keepBtn neutral); messages/en.json:5558 ('keepBtn': 'Keep'), messages/de.json:5568 ('keepBtn': 'Behalten'); app/[locale]/queue/[token]/page.tsx:46-47 ('noTip': 'Kein Trinkgeld, danke' / 'No tip, thanks'); messages/en.json:401,1565,2368 and messages/de.json:401,1575,2378 (plain 'No'/'Nein' throughout, no shaming variants found)

### dm-drip-pricing , Never reveal mandatory fees only after commitment (drip pricing)
- Reasoning: Drip pricing is the single largest documented abandonment driver for exactly this kind of transactional flow, and the reversed version (transparency) is the trust foundation a Swiss booking marketplace needs to compete against opaque phone/walk-in booking. The product already implements the anti-pattern's inverse as a T1-cited hard law, and the code matches: the running total renders live starting on the very first step (service selection), not first at payment.
- Law evidence: PSYCHOLOGY.md law 5 (lines 27-28): 'The full CHF total (service + surcharge + promo + credits) renders on every booking step, not first at Bezahlen... relative-only framing of a big charge measurably burns trust.' Hard line 3 (line 66) separately bans 'relative-only price framing that hides the absolute price.'
- Code evidence: components-legacy/booking/ServicesStaffStep.tsx:548 (live rolling total on step 1, formData.totalPrice); components-legacy/booking/PayConfirmStep.tsx:427-428,570-571,581 (same total repeated and itemized through to payment, no new mandatory line sprung late)

### dm-systemize-dont-improvise , Systemize, don't improvise: one rule set applied everywhere
- Reasoning: This is precisely LOCKFILE.md's stated reason for existing, in its own words, and it matters most for a product built by many parallel subagents rather than one consistent human hand: without a single enforced rule set, every surface is locally defensible but the whole product reads as stitched together by different designers, which is the exact failure this principle names. Solen goes further than most systems by making it a hard machine gate rather than a style guide nobody rereads.
- Law evidence: LOCKFILE.md lines 3-11 (verbatim): 'Without literal locked values, parallel subagents independently re-decide... hundreds of times... The result: every section is locally Fresha-accurate but the whole site feels stitched together by 14 different designers. This file factors all global decisions out of the parallel work.' RATIONALE.md line 294 (drift rule B5, no per-category branching) and LOCKFILE §1 closed-inventory color rule (DS-3) are the same principle applied to components and tokens respectively.
- Code evidence: .claude/hooks/pre-edit-drift-gate.sh (PreToolUse gate blocking hardcoded hex/arbitrary values outside the token set) and .claude/hooks/pre-edit-psychology-gate.py (same enforcement for the behavioral layer) mechanically enforce the one-rule-set-everywhere principle at edit time, not just in documentation; the solen-drift-check skill audits for residual drift

### dm-empty-state-match-cause , Empty states should match the treatment to the cause
- Reasoning: This is not a gap, it is a dated, named, owner-council decision (Q60, 2026-05-02, logged in _tasks/archive/SOLEN_DESIGN.archived.md) that explicitly bans the single generic empty state as an anti-pattern and mandates 3 distinct treatments per cause (first-run FTU / no-upcoming-inline / filtered-empty), each with its own icon, headline, copy and CTA tone. The search results surface (the highest-traffic empty-state case on a booking marketplace, since 'no salons found' is the moment users are most likely to bounce) goes further: a cause-detection function walks 5 distinct branches (no city supply -> nationwide search, date filter -> loosen date, other filters -> clear filters, query miss -> nearest category, generic fallback) and renders a distinct icon + headline + primary CTA + optional secondary link per branch, driven by a real API call so the recovery suggestions are never fabricated. Building a generic reusable 'smart empty state' component now would either duplicate this cause-detection logic or flatten it back into the one-size-fits-all pattern the owner already rejected.
- Law evidence: _tasks/archive/SOLEN_DESIGN.archived.md:589 (Q60, dated 2026-05-02: 3 treatments FTU/Inline/Filtered, explicit anti-pattern ban on 'single generic empty state used for all 3 contexts'); _design-system/COMPONENT_REGISTRY.md:115 (LoadingStates.md Pattern 2 grammar doc)
- Code evidence: app/[locale]/_components/search/SearchTemplate.tsx:2205-2312 (cause-aware EmptyState: 5 branches, each own Icon/headline/primary/secondary, fed by /api/search/no-results); components-legacy/ui/EmptyState.tsx:13-26 (doc comment pointing to the cause-specific FTU/Inline/Filtered variants); components-legacy/discovery/DiscoveryEmptyState.tsx:7-27 (Discovery's own cause: compass icon + reset-filters action)

### dm-empty-search-recent-searches , Empty search state should default to recent searches, not blank
- Reasoning: This exact pattern is fully built and wired into the primary search sheet: a localStorage-persisted, deduped, capped-at-5 recent-search list that renders as tappable chips the instant the query field gets focus, and a single tap restores the query/service/city and re-runs the search. For a repetitive-use surface like a beauty booking marketplace (same salon, same service, rebooked over weeks/months), this is exactly the recognition-over-recall win the principle argues for, and it is already the shipped behavior, not a gap.
- Law evidence: none
- Code evidence: app/[locale]/_components/homepage/useRecentSearches.ts:1-94 (solen.recentSearches localStorage hook, MAX=5, dedupe by query/service/city, push-on-submit only); app/[locale]/_components/search/SearchOverlay.tsx:384-393 (handleRecentClick re-applies query+city on tap), :503 (visibleRecents), :518,702-704 (rendered inside the inputFocused block, i.e. shown on focus, not after typing)

### dm-error-needs-exit , Every error needs an exit: at least one recovery action
- Reasoning: Every error surface in the design system already requires a recovery action at the type level, not as an afterthought. ErrorState.tsx makes onRetry a required prop (component cannot compile/render without one), and the route-level ErrorFallback additionally surfaces the raw error.message and an error.digest technical-detail line alongside the retry button. SearchTemplate has its own locked ErrorState with the same retry-required shape. There is no lone-OK dead-end pattern anywhere in these primitives to fix.
- Law evidence: _design-system/COMPONENT_REGISTRY.md:36 (ErrorState row: 'red icon chip + headline + subtext + ink Try again pill... so it never shows an indefinite spinner')
- Code evidence: components-legacy/ui/ErrorState.tsx:26 (onRetry: () => void is a required, not optional, prop) and :64-70 (rendered retry button); components-legacy/ui/ErrorFallback.tsx:31-42 (retry button + error.message + error.digest technical line); app/[locale]/_components/search/SearchTemplate.tsx:2319-2353 (search's own ErrorState, retry required + rendered)

### dm-placeholder-text-teaches , Placeholder text should teach, not just label
- Reasoning: The production search-sheet placeholder ('Service, Salon oder Stylist:in') already names the three matchable fields in the exact WHAT/HOW/SCOPE spirit of the principle, and multiple dashboard search inputs go further, one names a concrete example format ('Bestellnummer suchen, z.B. SOL-7K2QX'), others name the matchable field pair ('Name oder E-Mail suchen...'). The one bare placeholder found (homepage inline picker, 'Was suchst du?') sits directly above a row of visible, tappable service-category chips, so the scope is taught by a stronger recognition-based mechanism (visible chips) rather than placeholder text, which is consistent with the same underlying goal. There is no systemic gap here worth a new pattern; a targeted homepage copy tweak would be a one-line polish item, not a new component or system.
- Law evidence: none
- Code evidence: app/[locale]/_components/search/SearchOverlay.tsx:441,536 (queryPlaceholder = 'Service, Salon oder Stylist:in'); messages/de.json:4405 (searchPlaceholder: 'Bestellnummer suchen (z. B. SOL-7K2QX)'), :2412 ('Name oder E-Mail suchen...'), :4352 ('Styles suchen...'); app/[locale]/_components/homepage/SearchBar.tsx:433 (bare 'Was suchst du?', but :438-466 renders visible service chips directly beneath it)

### dm-presets-before-calendar , Date-range pickers should lead with presets, not a bare grid
- Reasoning: The one true date-range (from-to) picker in the codebase already leads with a preset-pill row above the custom-range inputs, exactly the structure the principle argues for: recognition-click presets first, manual range entry as the fallback below. The other date UI in the product (booking DateTimePicker, search Zeit filter) is single-date/single-slot selection, not a range, so it is out of scope for this range-picker principle and does not need the presets-first treatment applied to it (that would misapply a range-picker pattern to a single-date picker, which LOCKFILE explicitly keeps as one primitive, not to be forked).
- Law evidence: _design-system/COMPONENT_REGISTRY.md:55 (DateTimePicker is THE one date/time primitive, V3-D445, and explicitly notes range picking is out of its current scope, meaning the range-picker below is the correct separate surface, not a duplicate to consolidate)
- Code evidence: components-legacy/ui/DateRangePicker.tsx:38-93 (PRESETS array of 4 quick-range pills rendered under a 'quickRange' heading BEFORE the 'customRange' from/to date inputs); app/[locale]/dashboard/analytics/page.tsx:159 (live call-site, salon-owner analytics header)

### dm-hover-preview-vs-committed-value , Hover preview and committed value are independent layers that reset
- Reasoning: The underlying problem this principle solves (a live in-progress value must never be mistaken for the saved one) is already solved in Solen, just via the touch-appropriate equivalent: local drag state updates live while dragging and the actual committed value (URL param / onChange) fires only on pointer release, so there is never a moment where a stale in-progress value silently becomes the record of truth. A literal mouse-hover-preview layer would be solving a problem for pointer users this mobile-first product doesn't prioritize, and the one place a hover-preview-style star rating could apply (rate-your-visit) is a direct-tap control on a phone screen where the tapped star IS the value, immediately, by design, matching the reassurance-over-cleverness posture of a fast checkout-adjacent flow.
- Law evidence: none (no LOCKFILE/RATIONALE row names hover-preview layering directly; evaluated against the mobile-first product DNA in memory/user.md and the drag-then-commit pattern's own inline rationale comments)
- Code evidence: app/[locale]/_components/search/FilterSheet.tsx:62 comment 'Live label while dragging; commits to the URL only on release (pointer/key up)', implemented at lines 80-104 (PriceSlider: local val state during pointer move, onChange fires only in up()) and lines 167-201 (RatingBar: same split via idx/commit()). Contrast: app/[locale]/_components/primitives/RatingStars.tsx:85-100,137 (interactive star input used for rating a visit) has NO hover handler at all, onClick sets the value directly and immediately, confirming Solen's rating control is intentionally direct-commit, not preview-then-commit.

### dm-snap-slider-to-round-steps , Snap slider values to discrete round steps
- Reasoning: This is a straight, exact match already shipped and owner-approved, not a gap. Both sliders in the search filter sheet round the raw pointer position to a fixed step before it ever reaches state, so a user can never end up with an arbitrary value like the '47.3' example in the principle.
- Law evidence: none needed (implementation detail, no LOCKFILE row required it, but nothing conflicts either)
- Code evidence: app/[locale]/_components/search/FilterSheet.tsx:77-91 PriceSlider: MIN=20, MAX=300, STEP=10, fromX() computes 'Math.round((MIN + ratio * (MAX - MIN)) / STEP) * STEP' so every drag position snaps to a round CHF-10 value (comment at line 60-62 cites V3-D391/V3-D392 as owner-approved). Lines 147-201 RatingBar: RATING_STOPS=[null,3.0,3.5,4.0,4.5], idxFromX() does 'Math.round(ratio * last)' to snap to one of 5 discrete stops, with the fill/thumb easing visually to the settled stop (owner note in-code: 'don't snap' refers only to the animation curve, the VALUE itself is always discrete).

### dm-live-checklist-beats-post-submit-errors , Show requirements early: live checklist beats post-submit errors
- Reasoning: This exact pattern is built and shipping, not a gap. Reset-password's Requirement component renders three password rules live under the field, each flipping from grey to text-s-success with a check the instant it's met while typing, and the submit button stays disabled until every rule plus the confirm-match are true. For a Swiss booking product where a customer or a salon employee is often filling this in on a phone between other tasks, catching a rule before the tap costs nothing while a red error after submit costs a full round trip and re-entry. The one real gap is consistency, not invention: the account-creation flow (register/signup) checks the same three rules but only on submit, surfacing a toast instead of the checklist that already exists two files away in the same auth family - per the exists-check discipline that is an EXTEND, not a new build.
- Law evidence: _design-system/LOCKFILE.md:1345 (errors must name the exact cause, e.g. 'Password needs at least 8 characters', never a generic message) - compatible with the live-checklist pattern though it does not lock the checklist UI itself
- Code evidence: app/[locale]/auth/reset-password/page.tsx:169-175 (Requirement rows, live green check as user types), :194 (disabled={loading || !passwordValid || !passwordsMatch}), :214-221 (Requirement component definition). Gap to extend into: app/[locale]/auth/register/page.tsx:89-103 (same 3 rules, but toast-only, checked post-submit).

### dm-pagination-pattern-by-job , Pick the pagination pattern by the job
- Reasoning: Solen already routes each list to the pattern its job calls for, and no shared 'Pagination' component was ever built to force one pattern everywhere (confirmed: no file named *pagination* exists in app/lib/components). Search results are a 'stay in control while comparing options' job, so SearchTemplate ends every page with an explicit ink 'Load more' button that states the remaining count. Reviews are the same job at smaller scale, with their own 'Mehr laden' button. Inspo is the one genuinely passively-consumed feed with no location to return to, and it is the one surface built as auto-loading infinite scroll via IntersectionObserver. Numbered pagination appears nowhere, which is also the right call: there is no admin table in the product where an owner needs to jump straight to an arbitrary known page N.
- Law evidence: none - no LOCKFILE/RATIONALE row locks pagination-pattern choice; the match is emergent from independently correct implementation decisions, not a written rule
- Code evidence: app/[locale]/_components/search/SearchTemplate.tsx:1673-1702 (explicit Load more button with remaining-count label); app/[locale]/inspo/page.tsx:248-260 (IntersectionObserver-driven infinite scroll); app/[locale]/salon/[slug]/reviews/page.tsx (Mehr laden load-more button, referenced at lines 15/81)

### dm-inline-retry-failed-uploads , Keep a failed upload's state inline rather than resetting to zero
- Reasoning: The core claim, keep a failed upload's state inline instead of resetting the whole thing to zero, is already built and live. components-legacy/ui/ImageUpload.tsx (used by GalleryManager on the live /dashboard/gallery route) tracks each file as its own PendingFile card with retained file/progress/status; a failure only flips that one card to status:'error' with an inline overlay + filename + message, it never clears the other pending or already-uploaded cards and never resets the whole multi-file queue. Its sibling components-legacy/ui/ImageUploader.tsx goes further and already ships the literal 'fix costs what the fault cost' behavior: on failure it keeps the selected File in a ref and shows an explicit 'Nochmal versuchen' retry button that re-uploads the SAME file object with no OS file-picker round-trip. On top of both, the product has a LOCKED, registered generic pattern for this exact philosophy, components-legacy/ui/ErrorState.tsx ('any dashboard panel whose fetch can fail... ink Try again pill', COMPONENT_REGISTRY.md:36), so inline-retry-over-reset is already house law, not a gap to introduce. The one real delta, ImageUpload.tsx's per-card error overlay has no retry click handler (only remove), is a one-line parity fix mirroring code that already exists 40 lines away in ImageUploader.tsx, not a new pattern needing a mockup. Separately, Solen has no genuine large-file (video/multi-minute) upload surface, caps are 5-10MB images and a few-MB PDFs, so the principle's own motivating scenario (48MB, dies at 90%, minutes of lost transfer) doesn't have a live analog here to justify building new infrastructure for.
- Law evidence: _design-system/COMPONENT_REGISTRY.md:36 (ErrorState: 'Inline a fetch failed panel... ink Try again pill... any dashboard panel whose fetch can fail, so it never shows an indefinite spinner', locked) codifies inline-retry-over-reset as existing product law
- Code evidence: components-legacy/ui/ImageUpload.tsx:11-19 (PendingFile retains file/progress/status per item), :133-151 (failure sets status:'error' on just that item, others untouched), :293-298 & :364-380 (inline per-card error overlay + filename+message list, no full reset); components-legacy/ui/ImageUploader.tsx:29-31,187-198 (lastFileRef retains the File object; 'Nochmal versuchen' button re-calls handleFile(lastFileRef.current) with no re-pick); components-legacy/ui/ErrorState.tsx:1-30 (locked generic onRetry pattern)
- Mockup brief: none needed for a new build; if closing the small parity gap, it would be a mechanical one-line change (not a design ADD): give the error overlay in components-legacy/ui/ImageUpload.tsx:293-298 an onClick={() => uploadFile(item)} using the already-retained item.file, mirroring the retry button already shipping in components-legacy/ui/ImageUploader.tsx:190-198.

---

## Synthesis (ranked ADD queue, groupings, shaky-verdict notes)

# Synthesis: 217 Harvested Design Principles vs. Solen's Design Law

## 1. ADD list ,  ranked by product value

**1. `dm-length-beats-symbols` ,  Password rule is backwards on security AND friction**
Why: current client-side rule (8 chars + uppercase + digit) rejects long passphrases while accepting `Passwort1`; server schema doesn't even enforce composition, so the client gate is pure friction with zero security backing. This sits on signup/reset, a conversion-critical, security-critical surface.
Mockup: replace the 3-checkbox composition list on `register/page.tsx` and `reset-password/page.tsx` with one entropy-scored strength bar (reuse the existing `h-1.5` progress-bar treatment from `ImageUpload.tsx`) plus one exact-cause error line. Floor stays 8 chars to match the server schema.

**2. `dm-preserve-caret-on-autoformat` ,  Phone-field caret jumps to end on every keystroke**
Why: both live phone inputs (`GuestBookingForm.tsx`, `PayConfirmStep.tsx`) rebuild the whole string on each format pass with no caret math, so fixing a mistyped digit mid-number forces delete-and-retype. This is on the money path (checkout/booking contact field).
Mockup: none needed, behavior fix only. Capture selection position pre-format, remap post-format, restore via `setSelectionRange`.

**3. `dm-cursor-pagination-over-offset` ,  Inspo feed duplicates cards on scroll**
Why: `discovery_feed` is offset-paginated but actively appended to by a 30-min cron ingest; a new item inserted mid-scroll shifts every later page down one row, so the next auto-load repeats the last card the user already saw. Inspo is the product's designated retention/habit surface, so a visible glitch there costs more than elsewhere.
Mockup: switch `discovery_feed` to a keyset cursor over `(gender_rank, sort_order, created_at, id)`; demo a mid-scroll publish producing no repeat.

**4. `dp-crop-bone-shaft-not-joint` ,  Salon photo uploads have zero crop/joint framing guidance**
Why: portfolio photos (hair, nails, massage) get a blind center `object-cover` crop with no framing hint; an arbitrary crop through a wrist/elbow reads as amputation on a marketplace where PSYCHOLOGY law 7 makes the photo the decision input.
Mockup: one line of framing copy under `GalleryManager.tsx`'s drop zone + bias `object-position` to center-top on the square gallery grid.

**5. `dp-logo-format-earned-recognition` ,  Three mutually inconsistent legacy icon marks are live simultaneously**
Why: favicon (teal circle), PWA icons (maroon monogram on cream), and manifest theme colors (retired coral/cream) all predate the current locked ink/white wordmark and disagree with each other and with it.
Mockup: one small locked ink-on-white symbol for icon-constrained slots only (favicon/PWA/share preview); wordmark stays canonical everywhere else. Needs owner sign-off on the symbol itself before build (new brand asset, mockup-first law applies).

**6. `dm-two-months-range-picker` ,  Vacation Mode range has no calendar, no range preview**
Why: the one real cross-month range in the product (`VacationTab`) is two disconnected native `<input type=date>` boxes, and `DateTimePicker.md` already flags a range variant as "not yet built, add here when needed, don't fork" ,  this is that moment.
Mockup: extend `DateTimePicker.tsx` with a range variant, two adjacent months, shaded span, result pill; swap into `dashboard/settings` VacationTab.

**7. `dp-line-length-measure` ,  Owner already approved 68ch cap, never implemented**
Why: `TASTE_LOG.md` 2026-07-15 locked "cap long body/description text at 68ch," but no `ch`-based utility exists anywhere; `SalonAbout.tsx`'s "Über uns" text still runs unconstrained at `max-w-3xl` (100+ chars/line), the worst case given DE/FR/IT copy runs 20-35% longer than EN.
Mockup: swap `max-w-3xl` for `max-w-[68ch]` on the About description; promote to a shared `.prose-measure` utility if approved.

**8. `dp-irradiation-illusion` ,  Same salon name renders two different perceived weights on one page**
Why: `CategoryHeroCarousel.tsx`'s white-on-photo name ships at `font-bold` (700) while the ink-on-white name in the results grid directly below ships at 600 ,  this is also `RATIONALE.md`'s own open retrofit item 8 (stroke/weight inconsistency), unresolved.
Mockup: drop the white h3 to 600 to match; show side-by-side.

**9. `dp-stem-matched-icon-stroke` ,  Icon stroke width has two conflicting defaults, both admitted-unresolved**
Why: LOCKFILE locks 1.9 for inline glyphs AND 2/1.75 in the size-pairing table for the same monochrome system; `RATIONALE.md`'s own retrofit queue names this pair as inconsistent and unretrofitted.
Mockup: small calibrated stroke-by-size table (derived from Inter's measured stem at each locked type-role size) applied to FilterSheet chip icon, ProgressStepper icon, header hamburger.

**10. `dp-progress-motion-is-status-signal` ,  Admin bulk-import bar freezes indistinguishably from stalling**
Why: `ImportProgressBar.tsx` is pure width-transition with no independent activity signal; if the paginated TikTok import stalls between API pages, the bar visually freezes exactly like "done." Low blast radius (admin-only), cheap CSS-only fix.
Mockup: add a subtle motion cue (shimmer or pulse) distinct from the fill itself, so stalled ≠ frozen.

**11. `dp-grid-matches-content` ,  No named decision step for list vs. grid vs. modular vs. hierarchical layout**
Why: `RATIONALE.md`'s own gap register (G2) already flags this as missing, and the dashboard D1 clutter complaint (equal-weight modular grid on hierarchical content) already happened once from lacking exactly this check.
Mockup: not visual ,  a one-line classification step added before layout, demoed against the dashboard hero panel that already hit this failure.

**12. `dp-asymmetric-balance` ,  No balance/negative-space step in the taste-diagnosis walk**
Why: `RATIONALE.md` gap register (D1) already names the computable version as unbuilt; the owner has already hit this complaint by name on `BentoBusiness` ("what about the balance... gaps between the bento boxes") with no repeatable check to answer it.
Mockup: not visual ,  add a "collapse test" step (mentally remove darkest element, then biggest shape, then largest open space) to `solen-taste-diagnosis`, demoed against the live BentoBusiness asymmetric grid.

---

## 2. DONT_ADD ,  grouped by reason class

*(includes both DONT_ADD and CONFLICTS_WITH_LOCK verdicts ,  both are "do not build this" outcomes, ~57 items total. Grouped, not exhaustive per-item reasoning.)*

### Conflicts with a frozen literal / dated owner decision (32 items)
- **Elevation/shadow discipline**: `dp-blur-opacity-elevation-ladder`, `dp-two-layer-shadows`, `dp-edges-before-shadow`'s extension, `dp-shadow-color-inherits-surface-hue` ,  all fight the "shadow lives in exactly two places" lock and named, dated shadow-simplification decisions.
- **Radius system**: `dp-base-unit-radius-scale`, `dp-proportional-corner-radius`, `dp-root-2-rectangle`, `dp-radius-brand-personality-dial` ,  Solen's radius table is a hand-picked closed set (CONV, not formula-derived); pill/full-round tokens have no clean path through a proportional or base-unit system.
- **Color system**: `dp-60-30-10`, `dm-60-30-10-color-ratio`, `dp-hsl-tonal-scale-from-one-hue`, `dp-color-emotion-association`, `dp-color-temperature-depth-cue`, `dm-analogous-harmony-related-set`, `dm-monochromatic-component-hierarchy` ,  the 80/17 budget, OKLCH-not-HSL generation, and semantic-not-emotional hue assignment are all named-and-superseded folk rules with a dated internal replacement already in force.
- **Dark mode**: `dp-dark-mode-irradiation-compensation`, `dp-dark-mode-hct-hue-lock` ,  web dark mode was explicitly declined by the owner 2026-07-15 ("no dark mode," graveyarded); no surface exists to apply either principle to.
- **Typography**: `dp-visual-angle-arcminutes`, `dp-modular-type-scale` ,  the type scale is a council-stamped LOCKED, hand-tuned table; re-deriving it from a formula means reopening a frozen row without the owner naming it.
- **Brand/font**: `dp-typeface-class-emotional-signal`, `dp-imperfection-authenticity-signal` ,  a second/decorative typeface was tried and killed multiple times (Geist, Anton, Hanken, etc.); font lock is "two optical cuts of one family, no third face."
- **Navigation affordance**: `dp-chevron-navigation-only` ,  Solen already uses direction (right=navigate, down=disclose) on purpose across FAQItem, Select, filter pills; this would require ripping out working accordions.
- **Focus/interaction states**: `dp-every-interactive-state-needs-treatment` ,  hover/active/disabled are already built; the global focus ring was explicitly killed twice by the owner ("furious repeat"), a documented tradeoff, not an oversight.
- **Links**: `dp-links-need-non-color-signal` ,  resting underlines on blue links were explicitly locked OUT (v3.1, twice-litigated); see OWNER_CALL below for the narrow real gap inside this one.
- **Motion/gesture**: `dm-entrance-200-300ms` (420ms is a dated, gate-enforced, deliberately-tried-faster-and-rejected lock), `dm-press-compress-then-overshoot` (overshoot is earned by drag momentum only, never a discrete tap), `dm-fake-typing-duration` (fabricated status = banned, chat is dead anyway), `dm-variable-rewards-compulsive-recheck` (contradicts the deterministic-Inspo-ranking law and the below-habit-floor booking cadence), `dm-range-selection-hover-click-drag` (no date-range surface exists; single-select is locked), `dm-progress-numeric-plus-checkmark-stepper` (violates "one progress indicator per screen," already graveyarded once for booking).
- **Misc**: `dp-subitizing` (mobile tab bar is locked at 5, a deliberate decision, not a miscount), `dp-scan-path-f-z` (F/Z-pattern is a named MYTH in the folklore table), `dp-aspect-ratio-isolation-context` (bleeds into the just-killed full-bleed direction ,  see OWNER_CALL), `dm-golden-ratio-spacing` (golden ratio is a named MYTH, literally the gate's own should-block test case).

### Wrong for this product (no matching surface / architecture mismatch) (20 items)
- **No print pipeline exists**: `dp-rgb-cmyk-gamut-mismatch`, `dp-bleed-extend-past-trim`, `dp-icc-profile-screen-to-print`, `dp-spot-pantone-vs-cmyk`, `dp-manuscript-canon-1-1-2-2-margins` ,  five separate print-production principles with zero application; Solen has no press/paper/spine surface at all (only a browser-print invoice).
- **No matching content genre**: `dp-bouba-kiki` (every category name is a real word, not phonetically novel), `dp-semantic-form-checkable-fact` (no editorial-infographic surface, real charts already used), `dp-indexical-marks-physical-impression` (no physical brand collateral).
- **No live UI to attach to**: `dp-fake-vs-true-small-caps` (no small-caps usage of either kind exists), `dp-variable-font-size-conditional` (no variable font shipped), `dp-logo-clearspace-self-scaling` (four fixed logo sizes, no arbitrary-reproduction context), `dp-even-stroke-weights` (fights the scalable-SVG pipeline directly, and Solen's real stroke values are deliberately fractional), `dm-three-signal-drag-pickup` (no Kanban surface exists), `dm-paste-problem-strip-and-distribute` (no segmented code input exists), `dm-segmented-code-auto-advance` (phone-OTP backend is dormant, zero frontend callers), `dm-one-string-not-n-states` (same dormant OTP situation), `dm-strength-bar-false-progress` (no strength bar exists; the shipped checklist pattern already avoids this exact bug).
- **Payments architecture makes it moot**: `dp-icon-padding-compensation` (buttons already center icon+label as one flex unit, sidestepping the problem), `dm-card-network-first-digit-detection` is ALREADY_EXISTS not DONT_ADD ,  correction, this belongs in section 3, not here.
- **Ethics/marketplace-ownership mismatch**: `dp-decoy-effect` (Solen doesn't own salon pricing; manufacturing a dominated tier would mean either fabricating data or Solen unilaterally distorting a partner's real catalog ,  conflicts with the no-fabrication law and "comparability beats trimming" law).
- **Already solved more simply**: `dp-convincing-glass-five-parameters` (FROST_GLASS is locked and scoped; five-parameter refraction math is imperceptible at 40px icon-button scale and fights restraint DNA).

### Trend/technique without evidence, or a myth already named and rejected (5 items)
- `dp-serif-terminals-scan-anchors` ,  source material admits it's an unverified, hedged hypothesis that cuts against most reading-speed research; also fights the sans-serif-as-VALUE lock.
- `dp-unverifiable-timestamps-note` ,  not a design principle at all, a disclosure about missing source-video data; drop from the harvest set.
- `dm-adjacent-hues-gradient-stops` / `dm-gradient-angle-deliberate` ,  Solen's few gradients are functionally derived (legibility scrim, token-meaning), not wheel-math or default-angle driven; no brand-defining hero gradient exists to need a formal angle/hue-adjacency law.

## 4. OWNER_CALL ,  items where the verdict genuinely depends on a fresh owner decision

**`dp-aspect-ratio-isolation-context`** (Aspect ratio as an isolation-vs-context dial)
The isolation half (5:4 card crop) is already done. The context half (wider frame for environmental storytelling) bleeds directly into the full-bleed hero direction the owner killed on sight one day before this evaluation ("i told you full bleed is not okay... the mockup is denied"). **Recommendation: do not add.** If the owner wants a context-widening treatment later, it needs to be scoped explicitly as NOT full-bleed and re-approved by name ,  don't let this ride back in under a different label.

**`dp-links-need-non-color-signal`** (Links need a non-color signal, not just color)
The blanket ask (resting underlines on all blue links) directly reopens a twice-litigated, dated lock (v3.1: "NO UNDERLINES... blue inline links also sit bare at rest"). But a real, narrow instance of the underlying WCAG 1.4.1 concern exists: 2-3 inline links in `onboarding/salon/page.tsx` (ToS/Privacy) render as `text-s-accent hover:underline` with no weight differentiator at rest, meaning on touch (no hover) they're distinguished from prose by color alone ,  which SOURCE.md itself already bans as an anti-pattern. **Recommendation: fix those 2-3 specific links by adding `font-medium`/`font-semibold` (matching the pattern other blue links like `FlowBar.tsx` already use), do not touch the underline lock.**

---

## 5. Quality note ,  verdicts that deserve a second read

1. **Four separate "ALREADY_EXISTS" verdicts lean on the same single paragraph.** `dp-optical-weight-circle-square`, `dp-optical-cap-height-alignment`, `dp-optical-centering-icon-design`, and `dp-optical-vs-geometric-center` all cite the identical 2026-07-15 TASTE_LOG round-1 sentence as their evidence. That decision is real, but it's a one-line owner adoption with **zero code implementation** (confirmed: no overshoot/nudge/baseline-offset code exists anywhere per each entry's own codeEvidence). Four principles being marked "already exists" off one un-implemented sentence is optimistic bucketing ,  this is really one open retrofit item, not four closed ones. Worth re-reading as a single ADD-flavored "implement the queued optical corrections" item rather than four ALREADY_EXISTS verdicts.

2. **`dm-determinate-progress-only-when-computable`** surfaced a real bug (fabricated upload percentages in `ImageUpload.tsx`) but was still verdicted ALREADY_EXISTS because the *law* banning fabrication exists. The law existing doesn't mean the code complies ,  this is a live violation of an existing rule, arguably higher priority than most of the ADD list. Same pattern in `dp-information-scent-link-labels` (weak-scent "Mehr erfahren" copy still shipping) and `dm-shimmer-follows-reading-direction` (legacy reversed-direction shimmer still live in `SearchTemplate.tsx`). These three should probably be pulled into a short "law exists, code doesn't comply" punch list rather than resting inside ALREADY_EXISTS, since the practical action item is identical to an ADD (go fix code) even though no new principle needs adopting.

3. **`dp-decoy-effect` reasoning leans hard on an ethics framing** ("Solen unilaterally distorting a salon's real catalog") to reach DONT_ADD, but the harvested principle as commonly practiced (surfacing three real tiers where the middle option is deliberately positioned to look best) doesn't require fabricating anything ,  it's a presentation-order question about salons' own existing bundle data. The verdict's conclusion (don't build Solen-side decoy engineering) is probably still right given the marketplace doesn't own pricing, but the ethics-taxonomy citation (DSA Art. 25) may be overreaching for what is, in the harvested principle's mild form, closer to "let real options be presented well" than a dark pattern. Worth a second read if this becomes relevant to any future pricing-display work.

4. **`dp-progress-numeric-plus-checkmark-stepper` (CONFLICTS_WITH_LOCK) contains its own contradiction in the code evidence**: it correctly rejects adding a numeric-fraction layer on top of the queue stepper, citing "one progress indicator per screen" ,  but then notes in a parenthetical that the *same* queue page currently renders both the stepper AND a separate numeric time bar simultaneously, which is a live violation of the very rule the verdict just used to reject the harvested principle. That's a real bug flagged almost as an aside; it deserves its own ticket, not a footnote inside an unrelated verdict.

5. **The print-design cluster (5 DONT_ADD verdicts: CMYK/bleed/ICC/Pantone/manuscript-margins)** are all correctly rejected, but their presence signals the harvesting pipeline pulled in principles from a print/publication-design corpus with no relevance-filtering against "this is a web/mobile SaaS marketplace." Not a verdict-quality problem, but worth flagging upstream: roughly 8-10% of the 217-item harvest (print color science, physical packaging, book-spread margins) never had a chance of applying here, and a scope filter on the harvest step would save review cycles next time.

6. **Two ADD items carry speculative severity language without a documented real-world incident**: `dp-crop-bone-shaft-not-joint` ("actively hurts trust") and `dm-cursor-pagination-over-offset` ("a small but real trust-eroding glitch") are both reasoned from mechanism, not from an observed complaint or support ticket. The mechanism reasoning is sound and the code evidence for the underlying architecture gap is solid, but if triaging against limited build capacity, verify severity against actual salon-photo quality complaints or Inspo scroll-repeat reports before prioritizing above items with harder evidence (like the password/caret items, which are provably wrong today regardless of user reports).
